# Notices

Most blocks in `catalogue-items/` are our own work. These are adapted from openly licensed sources, and the originals keep their licences.

| Block | Adapted from | Licence |
|---|---|---|
| `general/landing-page-design-planner` | Anthropic `frontend-design` skill, https://github.com/anthropics/skills (commit dbd4588) and Impeccable by Paul Bakaus, https://github.com/pbakaus/impeccable (commit d631a88) | Apache License 2.0 |
| `ecommerce/product-page-screenshot-auditor` | REA "reverse-engineer-anything" by morluto, https://github.com/morluto/rea (commit a9ee236) | MIT |
| `general/video-ad-edit-planner`, `general/social-video-edit-planner` | Creator OS Starter editing techniques (KevBuildsApps) | MIT |

The engineering framework (`docs/planning/10_AI_ENGINEERING_FRAMEWORK.md`) is built on the ideas in Chip Huyen's *AI Engineering* (O'Reilly, 2025), written in our own words.
